     1 // Aggressive Detector Service — adapter for High-Sensitivity Analysis (Strict screen)
     2 // Calibrated to aggressively detect and score AI text high while flagging subtle AI patterns.
     3 //
     4 // Leverages both the SEO Assistant's analyzeAIRisk engine and the full multi-layer
     5 // detection ensemble (analyzeAdvancedText) to ensure aggressive sensitivity and accurate high scores.
     6 
     7 import { analyzeAIRisk } from '@/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';
     8 import { analyzeAdvancedText } from './engine';
     9 import type { BalancedDetectorResult } from './balancedDetectorService';
    10 
    11 export interface AggressiveDetectorResult {
    12   // Scores (0–100) — exactly two categories (no Mixed category on this card)
    13   ai: number;
    14   human: number;
    15   risk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High';
    16   // Recommendations returned for actionable improvement
    17   recommendations: string[];
    18 }
    19 
    20 export interface AggressiveDetectorError {
    21   message: string;
    22   canRetry: boolean;
    23 }
    24 
    25 // runAggressiveDetector performs High-Sensitivity Analysis (Strict screen flagging weaker AI-like patterns).
    26 export async function runAggressiveDetector(
    27   text: string,
    28   balancedResult?: BalancedDetectorResult
    29 ): Promise<AggressiveDetectorResult> {
    30   const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);
    31 
    32   // If balancedResult was not provided, run the comprehensive multi-layer ensemble engine directly
    33   let balanced = balancedResult;
    34   if (!balanced && text.trim().length >= 25) {
    35     try {
    36       const adv = await analyzeAdvancedText(text);
    37       balanced = {
    38         ai: adv.overall.aiProbability,
    39         human: adv.overall.humanProbability,
    40         mixed: adv.overall.mixedProbability,
    41         verdict: adv.overall.verdict,
    42         risk: adv.overall.riskLevel,
    43         confidence: adv.overall.confidence,
    44         confidenceLevel: adv.overall.confidenceLevel,
    45         language: adv.language.primary?.name || 'English',
    46         engineVersion: adv.metadata.detectorVersion,
    47         modelVersion: adv.metadata.modelVersion,
    48         calibrationVersion: adv.metadata.calibrationVersion,
    49         languagePipelineVersion: adv.metadata.languagePipelineVersion,
    50         requestId: adv.metadata.requestId,
    51         analyzedAt: adv.metadata.analyzedAt,
    52         full: adv,
    53       };
    54     } catch {
    55       // Fallback cleanly to heuristic if advanced engine encounters error
    56     }
    57   }
    58 
    59   const baseAi = balanced?.ai ?? 0;
    60   const mixedSignal = balanced?.mixed ?? 0;
    61   const verdict = balanced?.verdict ?? '';
    62   const confidence = balanced?.confidence ?? 50;
    63   const isHighConfidence = balanced?.confidenceLevel !== 'Low' && confidence >= 35;
    64 
    65   // High-Sensitivity Strict Mode:
    66   // Designed as an aggressive screen to completely detect and score AI text high.
    67   let strictAi = raw.aiScore;
    68 
    69   const isClearAi =
    70     raw.aiScore >= 65 ||
    71     (isHighConfidence && baseAi >= 50) ||
    72     verdict === 'likely-ai' ||
    73     (baseAi >= 35 && raw.aiScore >= 45);
    74 
    75   const isModerateAi =
    76     raw.aiScore >= 40 ||
    77     (isHighConfidence && (baseAi >= 28 || mixedSignal >= 15)) ||
    78     (baseAi >= 25 && raw.aiScore >= 35);
    79 
    80   if (isClearAi) {
    81     // Clear AI text is aggressively detected and scored high (85%–98% AI, High Risk)
    82     strictAi = Math.max(
    83       raw.aiScore,
    84       Math.round(baseAi * 1.35 + mixedSignal * 0.85 + 16)
    85     );
    86     strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(85, strictAi));
    87   } else if (isModerateAi) {
    88     // Moderate/hybrid AI signals are strictly elevated to 52%–84%
    89     strictAi = Math.max(
    90       raw.aiScore,
    91       Math.round(baseAi * 1.25 + mixedSignal * 0.75 + 10)
    92     );
    93     strictAi = Math.min(84, Math.max(52, strictAi));
    94   } else {
    95     // Pure human writing with low balanced AI and human markers remains low
    96     strictAi = Math.max(
    97       raw.aiScore,
    98       Math.round(baseAi * 0.6 + mixedSignal * 0.5)
    99     );
   100     strictAi = Math.min(32, strictAi);
   101   }
   102 
   103   // Ensure invariant: strict AI can never be lower than verified balanced AI
   104   if (balancedResult || isHighConfidence) {
   105     strictAi = Math.max(strictAi, baseAi);
   106   }
   107   strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(4, strictAi));
   108 
   109   const finalHumanScore = 100 - strictAi;
   110   const finalRisk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High' =
   111     strictAi >= 65 ? 'High' : strictAi >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';
   112 
   113   const recommendations = [...raw.recommendations];
   114   if (mixedSignal > 10 && !recommendations.some((r) => r.includes('mixed') || r.includes('Hybrid'))) {
   115     recommendations.unshift('Hybrid AI-assisted phrasing detected across sentences; strict screen flagged weaker AI traces.');
   116   }
   117 
   118   return {
   119     ai: strictAi,
   120     human: finalHumanScore,
   121     risk: finalRisk,
   122     recommendations,
   123   };
   124 }
   125

   300 function confidenceLevel(score: number): 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High' | 'Very High' {
   301   if (score >= 85) return 'Very High';
   302   if (score >= 65) return 'High';
   303   if (score >= 40) return 'Medium';
   304   return 'Low';
   305 }
   306 
   307 function riskLevel(verdict: Verdict, aiProbability: number): 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High' | 'Critical' {
   308   if (verdict === 'likely-ai') return aiProbability >= 85 ? 'Critical' : 'High';
   309   if (verdict === 'mostly-ai-human-edited') return 'High';
   310   if (verdict === 'mixed' || verdict === 'mostly-human-ai-assisted') return 'Medium';
   311   if (verdict === 'insufficient-text' || verdict === 'inconclusive') return 'Low';
   312   return 'Low';
   313 }

import { analyzeAIRisk } from './src/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';
import { analyzeAdvancedText } from './src/lib/detection/engine';

const butterflySample = \`The seasonal migration of monarch butterflies spans thousands of miles between Canada and central Mexico.
Generations succeed one another along the journey, with no single insect completing the round trip.
Scientists still study how these fragile creatures orient themselves using the angle of the sun and the Earth's magnetic field.\`;

const chatGptSample = \`Artificial intelligence has revolutionized the way businesses operate in the modern era. The implementation of machine learning algorithms enables companies to process vast amounts of data with unprecedented efficiency. Furthermore, natural language processing capabilities allow for automated content generation that is indistinguishable from human writing. Organizations leveraging these technologies experience substantial improvements in productivity metrics and operational cost reduction. The utilization of AI-powered tools represents a paradigm shift in how enterprises approach problem-solving and decision-making processes.\`;

// User sample from database:
const userSimulatedBalanced = {
  ai: 28,
  human: 59,
  mixed: 13,
  verdict: 'mostly-human-ai-assisted',
  confidence: 44,
  confidenceLevel: 'Medium',
  full: {
    metadata: {
      classProbabilities: {
        ai: 0.634,
        human: 0.319,
        mixed: 0.001,
        'translated-ai': 0.027,
        'human-edited-ai': 0.019
      }
    }
  }
};

function computeStrict(text, balanced) {
  const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);
  const baseAi = balanced?.ai ?? raw.aiScore;
  const mixedSignal = balanced?.mixed ?? 0;
  const verdict = balanced?.verdict ?? '';
  const confidence = balanced?.confidence ?? 50;

  const classProbs = balanced?.full?.metadata?.classProbabilities || (balanced?.full as any)?.classProbabilities;
  const classifierAi = classProbs
    ? Math.round(((classProbs.ai ?? 0) + (classProbs['translated-ai'] ?? 0) + (classProbs['human-edited-ai'] ?? 0)) * 100)
    : 0;

  const combinedSignal = baseAi + mixedSignal * 0.75;

  const hasStrongAiPresence =
    classifierAi >= 45 ||
    raw.aiScore >= 60 ||
    baseAi >= 40 ||
    (baseAi >= 25 && (mixedSignal >= 10 || classifierAi >= 30 || verdict.includes('ai'))) ||
    verdict === 'likely-ai' ||
    verdict === 'mostly-ai-human-edited';

  const hasModerateAiPresence =
    classifierAi >= 25 ||
    raw.aiScore >= 35 ||
    baseAi >= 22 ||
    combinedSignal >= 25 ||
    mixedSignal >= 15;

  let strictAi = raw.aiScore;
  if (hasStrongAiPresence) {
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.15 + 8) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.45 + mixedSignal * 0.9 + 18)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(85, candidate));
  } else if (hasModerateAiPresence) {
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.1) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.25 + mixedSignal * 0.8 + 10)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(84, Math.max(50, candidate));
  } else {
    strictAi = Math.min(28, Math.max(raw.aiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 0.6 + mixedSignal * 0.4)));
  }

  const finalHuman = 100 - strictAi;
  const finalRisk = strictAi >= 65 ? 'High' : strictAi >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';
  return { strictAi, finalHuman, finalRisk, hasStrongAiPresence, hasModerateAiPresence, classifierAi };
}

async function run() {
  console.log('User sample result:', computeStrict('some text', userSimulatedBalanced));
  
  const butterflyAdv = await analyzeAdvancedText(butterflySample);
  console.log('Butterfly sample result:', computeStrict(butterflySample, {
    ai: butterflyAdv.overall.aiProbability,
    human: butterflyAdv.overall.humanProbability,
    mixed: butterflyAdv.overall.mixedProbability,
    verdict: butterflyAdv.overall.verdict,
    confidence: butterflyAdv.overall.confidence,
    confidenceLevel: butterflyAdv.overall.confidenceLevel,
    full: butterflyAdv
  }));

  const chatGptAdv = await analyzeAdvancedText(chatGptSample);
  console.log('ChatGPT sample result:', computeStrict(chatGptSample, {
    ai: chatGptAdv.overall.aiProbability,
    human: chatGptAdv.overall.humanProbability,
    mixed: chatGptAdv.overall.mixedProbability,
    verdict: chatGptAdv.overall.verdict,
    confidence: chatGptAdv.overall.confidence,
    confidenceLevel: chatGptAdv.overall.confidenceLevel,
    full: chatGptAdv
  }));
}
run();

   280 export async function classifyWithClassifier(text: string, languageCode: string): Promise<ClassifierResult> {
   281   const model = await getModel(languageCode);
   282   const binaryModel = await getBinaryModel(languageCode);
   283   if (!model || model.trainingSamples < 50) {
   284     return {
   285       available: false,
   286       aiProbability: 50,
   287       confidence: 0,
   288       chunkScores: [],
   289       classifierVersion: (await loadPackage()).version,
   290       languageModel: languageCode,
   291       classProbabilities: {},
   292     };
   293   }
   294 
   295   const vocabMap = VOCAB_INDEX[languageCode.toLowerCase()];
   296   const binaryVocabMap = binaryModel ? BINARY_VOCAB_INDEX[languageCode.toLowerCase()] : undefined;
   297   const chunks = splitChunks(text);
   298   const scored: ClassifierChunkScore[] = [];
   299   const classSums: Record<string, number> = {};
   300   let weightedAiSum = 0;
   301   let totalWeight = 0;
   302 
   303   for (const chunk of chunks) {
   304     const classProbs = scoreClasses(chunk.text, model, vocabMap);
   305     const weight = chunk.text.length;
   306     const binaryAiProb = binaryModel && binaryVocabMap
   307       ? scoreBinaryChunk(chunk.text, binaryModel, binaryVocabMap)
   308       : Object.values(classProbs).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) - (classProbs.human || 0);
   309     weightedAiSum += binaryAiProb * weight;
   310     totalWeight += weight;
   311     for (const [cls, p] of Object.entries(classProbs)) {
   312       classSums[cls] = (classSums[cls] || 0) + p * weight;
   313     }
   314     scored.push({
   315       start: chunk.start,
   316       end: chunk.end,
   317       aiProbability: Math.round(binaryAiProb * 100),
   318       classProbabilities: classProbs,
   319       textPreview: chunk.text.substring(0, 80).replace(/\s+/g, ' '),
   320     });
   321   }
   322 
   323   const total = totalWeight || 1;
   324   const aggregateClassProbs: Record<string, number> = {};
   325   for (const [cls, s] of Object.entries(classSums)) {

Organizations leveraging these technologies experience substantial improvements in productivity metrics and operational cost reduction. The utilization of AI-powered tools represents a paradigm shift in how enterprises approach problem-solving and decision-making processes.\`;

function evaluateStrict(text, balanced) {
  const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);
  const baseAi = balanced?.ai ?? raw.aiScore;
  const mixedSignal = balanced?.mixed ?? 0;
  const verdict = balanced?.verdict ?? '';
  const confidence = balanced?.confidence ?? 50;

  const classProbs = balanced?.full?.metadata?.classProbabilities || (balanced?.full as any)?.classProbabilities;
  const classifierAi = classProbs
    ? Math.round(((classProbs.ai ?? 0) + (classProbs['translated-ai'] ?? 0) + (classProbs['human-edited-ai'] ?? 0)) * 100)
    : 0;

  const combinedAiSignal = baseAi + mixedSignal * 0.75;
  const hasVerifiableAiAssistance =
    verdict.includes('ai') || verdict.includes('mixed') || verdict === 'mostly-ai-human-edited';

  // High-Sensitivity Strict Screening:
  // Decisive AI presence:
  const isStrictAi =
    raw.aiScore >= 60 ||
    classifierAi >= 38 ||
    baseAi >= 24 ||
    combinedAiSignal >= 26 ||
    (hasVerifiableAiAssistance && (baseAi >= 18 || classifierAi >= 25)) ||
    verdict === 'likely-ai';

  // Moderate/Subtle AI traces:
  const isModerateAi =
    raw.aiScore >= 35 ||
    classifierAi >= 20 ||
    baseAi >= 16 ||
    combinedAiSignal >= 18 ||
    mixedSignal >= 12;

  let strictAi = raw.aiScore;
  if (isStrictAi) {
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.15 + 10) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.5 + mixedSignal * 0.9 + 20)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(86, candidate));
  } else if (isModerateAi) {
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.1) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.25 + mixedSignal * 0.75 + 10)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(84, Math.max(52, candidate));
  } else {
    // Verified pure human writing
    strictAi = Math.min(26, Math.max(raw.aiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 0.5 + mixedSignal * 0.3)));
  }

  const finalHuman = 100 - strictAi;
  const finalRisk = strictAi >= 65 ? 'High' : strictAi >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';
  return { strictAi, finalHuman, finalRisk, classifierAi, baseAi, mixedSignal, isStrictAi };
}

   859 export function analyzeAIRisk(text: string): AIRiskResult {
   860   if (!text.trim()) return { humanScore: 0, aiScore: 0, riskLevel: 'Low', recommendations: [] };
   861 
   862   const sentences = getSentences(text);
   863   const words = getWords(text);
   864   const totalWords = words.length;
   865 
   866   if (totalWords < 6) {
   867     return {
   868       humanScore: 80,
   869       aiScore: 20,
   870       riskLevel: 'Low',
   871       recommendations: ['Provide a longer sample (at least 25 words) for in-depth AI risk inspection.'],
   872     };
   873   }
   874 
   875   const lengths = sentences.map((s) => getWords(s).length).filter((l) => l > 0);
   876   const count = lengths.length || 1;
   877   const avgLen = lengths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / count;
   878 
   879   // 1. Sentence length variance and Coefficient of Variation (Burstiness)
   880   const variance = lengths.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Math.pow(l - avgLen, 2), 0) / count;
   881   const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);
   882   const cv = avgLen > 0 ? stdDev / avgLen : 0;
   883 
   884   // Consecutive sentence length difference (human prose has dynamic rhythm)
   885   let consecutiveDeltaSum = 0;
   886   for (let i = 1; i < lengths.length; i++) {
   887     consecutiveDeltaSum += Math.abs(lengths[i] - lengths[i - 1]);
   888   }
   889   const avgConsecutiveDelta = lengths.length > 1 ? consecutiveDeltaSum / (lengths.length - 1) : 0;
   890 
   891   // 2. Lexical diversity (Type-Token Ratio)
   892   const cleanWords = words
   893     .map((w) => w.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''))
   894     .filter((w) => w.length > 1);
   895   const uniqueWordCount = new Set(cleanWords).size;
   896   const ttr = cleanWords.length > 0 ? uniqueWordCount / cleanWords.length : 0.5;
   897 
   898   // 3. Conversational / human punctuation (question marks, exclamation marks)
   899   const questionCount = (text.match(/\?/g) || []).length;

import { analyzeAdvancedText } from './src/lib/detection/engine';
import { analyzeAIRisk } from './src/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';

const userTelemetryBalanced = {
  ai: 28,
  human: 59,
  mixed: 13,
  verdict: 'mostly-human-ai-assisted',
  risk: 'Medium',
  confidence: 44,
  confidenceLevel: 'Medium',
  language: 'English',
  engineVersion: '2.5.1',
  modelVersion: 'ensemble-v4-classifier-v2',
  calibrationVersion: 'cal-v3-five-class',
  languagePipelineVersion: 'lang-v3',
  requestId: 'det-1790421427023-7zay',
  analyzedAt: '2026-09-26T11:17:07.023Z',
  full: {
    metadata: {
      classProbabilities: {
        ai: 0.634,
        human: 0.319,
        mixed: 0.001,
        'translated-ai': 0.027,
        'human-edited-ai': 0.019,
      },
    },
  },
};

const humanSample = \`The seasonal migration of monarch butterflies spans thousands of miles between Canada and central Mexico.
Generations succeed one another along the journey, with no single insect completing the round trip.
Scientists still study how these fragile creatures orient themselves using the angle of the sun and the Earth's magnetic field.\`;

const chatGptSample = \`Artificial intelligence has revolutionized the way businesses operate in the modern era. The implementation of machine learning algorithms enables companies to process vast amounts of data with unprecedented efficiency. Furthermore, natural language processing capabilities allow for automated content generation that is indistinguishable from human writing. Organizations leveraging these technologies experience substantial improvements in productivity metrics and operational cost reduction. The utilization of AI-powered tools represents a paradigm shift in how enterprises approach problem-solving and decision-making processes.\`;

function runStrict(text, balanced) {
  const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);
  const baseAi = balanced?.ai ?? 0;
  const mixedSignal = balanced?.mixed ?? 0;
  const verdict = balanced?.verdict ?? '';
  const confidence = balanced?.confidence ?? 50;

  const fullResult = balanced?.full;
  const classProbs =
    fullResult?.metadata?.classProbabilities ||
    fullResult?.classProbabilities;
  const classifierAi = classProbs
    ? Math.round(
        ((classProbs.ai ?? 0) +
          (classProbs['translated-ai'] ?? 0) +
          (classProbs['human-edited-ai'] ?? 0)) *
          100
      )
    : 0;

  const sentences = fullResult?.sentences || [];
  const aiSentenceCount = sentences.filter(
    (s) => s.verdict && (s.verdict.includes('ai') || s.verdict === 'mixed')
  ).length;
  const sentenceAiRatio =
    sentences.length > 0 ? (aiSentenceCount / sentences.length) * 100 : 0;

  const combinedAiSignal = baseAi + mixedSignal * 0.75;
  const hasVerifiableAiAssistance =
    verdict.includes('ai') ||
    verdict.includes('mixed') ||
    verdict === 'mostly-ai-human-edited' ||
    verdict === 'mostly-human-ai-assisted';

  const isHighConfidence =
    balanced?.confidenceLevel !== 'Low' && confidence >= 40;

  const isStrictAi =
    raw.aiScore >= 60 ||
    (isHighConfidence && (
      classifierAi >= 35 ||
      baseAi >= 22 ||
      combinedAiSignal >= 24 ||
      (hasVerifiableAiAssistance && (baseAi >= 16 || classifierAi >= 25 || mixedSignal >= 8)) ||
      sentenceAiRatio >= 20 ||
      verdict === 'likely-ai' ||
      verdict === 'mostly-ai-human-edited'
    ));

  const isModerateAi =
    raw.aiScore >= 35 ||
    (isHighConfidence && (
      classifierAi >= 18 ||
      baseAi >= 15 ||
      combinedAiSignal >= 18 ||
      mixedSignal >= 12 ||
      sentenceAiRatio >= 10
    ));

  let strictAi;
  if (isStrictAi) {
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.15 + 10) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.5 + mixedSignal * 0.9 + 20)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(86, candidate));
  } else if (isModerateAi) {
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.1) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.25 + mixedSignal * 0.75 + 10)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(84, Math.max(52, candidate));
  } else {
    strictAi = Math.min(
      26,
      Math.max(raw.aiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 0.5 + mixedSignal * 0.3))
    );
  }

  const risk = strictAi >= 65 ? 'High' : strictAi >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';
  return { strictAi, human: 100 - strictAi, risk };
}

async function main() {
  console.log('User sample strict:', runStrict('User text', userTelemetryBalanced));

  const advHuman = await analyzeAdvancedText(humanSample);
  console.log('Human butterfly strict:', runStrict(humanSample, {
    ai: advHuman.overall.aiProbability,
    human: advHuman.overall.humanProbability,
    mixed: advHuman.overall.mixedProbability,
    verdict: advHuman.overall.verdict,
    confidence: advHuman.overall.confidence,
    confidenceLevel: advHuman.overall.confidenceLevel,
    full: advHuman
  }));

  const advChat = await analyzeAdvancedText(chatGptSample);
  console.log('ChatGPT strict:', runStrict(chatGptSample, {
    ai: advChat.overall.aiProbability,
    human: advChat.overall.humanProbability,
    mixed: advChat.overall.mixedProbability,
    verdict: advChat.overall.verdict,
    confidence: advChat.overall.confidence,
    confidenceLevel: advChat.overall.confidenceLevel,
    full: advChat
  }));
}
main();
"

import { analyzeAdvancedText } from './src/lib/detection/engine';

const humanArticle = \`The development of urban transit infrastructure in twentieth-century metropolitan areas fundamentally altered residential patterns and economic mobility across North America. Prior to the widespread adoption of electric streetcars and grade-separated rapid transit, cities were characterized by dense pedestrian corridors centered around waterfront docks and rail terminals. As transit networks expanded into peripheral farmland, new residential suburbs emerged, creating the modern commuter landscape.

Municipal governments faced substantial logistical challenges during this rapid transition. Financing capital-intensive tunneling projects required innovative municipal bonding mechanisms and public-private partnerships. In cities like Boston and New York, private transit corporations initially operated competing subway and elevated lines before municipal consolidation became necessary to maintain uniform fares and coordinated transfer hubs. The physical construction itself demanded extensive cut-and-cover excavation through heavily populated commercial districts, disrupting surface traffic for years at a time.

Engineers and urban planners played a crucial role in designing ventilation shafts, subterranean drainage networks, and electrical substations capable of powering multi-car trainsets. The introduction of third-rail power distribution systems resolved many earlier safety concerns associated with overhead wires in underground tunnels. At the same time, signal systems evolved from basic manual block signaling to automated electro-pneumatic interlocking, drastically reducing headway times and preventing catastrophic rear-end collisions on high-frequency trunk lines.

Despite these engineering triumphs, transit expansion was never socially neutral. The placement of elevated tracks often depressed adjacent property values and subjected working-class neighborhoods to persistent noise and soot. Decades later, urban highway construction would repeat and amplify these patterns of displacement. Nevertheless, early rapid transit systems remain the indispensable backbone of modern metropolitan economies, carrying millions of daily passengers and providing a sustainable alternative to automobile congestion.\`;

  console.log('adv signals:', adv.signals);
  console.log('adv metadata:', adv.metadata);
}

    62 // computeAdjustedAiRisk
    63 //
    64 // Computes a single "adjusted AI risk" score that is used ONLY for verdict
    65 // classification. It is never returned as a replacement for the displayed
    66 // aiProbability / humanProbability / mixedProbability — those raw calibrated
    67 // scores remain unchanged.
    68 //
    69 // Rationale: Mixed is not a neutral bucket. Research-grade mixed content
    70 // (AI paragraphs + human paragraphs) still carries substantial AI risk.
    71 // A document with AI=45, Mixed=35, Human=20 has an adjusted AI risk of ~66
    72 // (with mixedAiContribution=0.6) and must NOT be labelled Human.
    73 //
    74 // mixedAiContribution is calibrated at 0.6 — validated against samples where:
    75 //   AI-generated text lightly edited  → Mixed 30-50%, AI 40-55%  → adjusted ≥65% → correctly AI
    76 //   True mixed (half/half paragraphs) → Mixed 30-45%, AI 25-40%  → adjusted 40-65% → correctly Mixed
    77 //   Human text polished by AI         → Mixed 20-35%, AI 15-30%  → adjusted 25-50% → correctly Human/Mixed

import { analyzeAIRisk } from './src/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';

const userTelemetryBalanced = {

  mixed: 13,
  verdict: 'mostly-human-ai-assisted',
  risk: 'Medium',
  confidence: 44,
  confidenceLevel: 'Medium',
  language: 'English',

  ai: 12,
  human: 85,

  verdict: 'likely-human',
  risk: 'Low',
  confidence: 68,
  confidenceLevel: 'High',
  language: 'English',

const humanSample = \`The development of urban transit infrastructure in twentieth-century metropolitan areas fundamentally altered residential patterns and economic mobility across North America. Prior to the widespread adoption of electric streetcars and grade-separated rapid transit, cities were characterized by dense pedestrian corridors centered around waterfront docks and rail terminals. As transit networks expanded into peripheral farmland, new residential suburbs emerged, creating the modern commuter landscape.

Municipal governments faced substantial logistical challenges during this rapid transition. Financing capital-intensive tunneling projects required innovative municipal bonding mechanisms and public-private partnerships. In cities like Boston and New York, private transit corporations initially operated competing subway and elevated lines before municipal consolidation became necessary to maintain uniform fares and coordinated transfer hubs. The physical construction itself demanded extensive cut-and-cover excavation through heavily populated commercial districts, disrupting surface traffic for years at a time.\`;

function evalStrict(text, balanced) {
  const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);
  const baseAi = balanced?.ai ?? 0;
  const mixedSignal = balanced?.mixed ?? 0;
  const verdict = balanced?.verdict ?? '';
  const confidence = balanced?.confidence ?? 50;

  const fullResult = balanced?.full;
  const classProbs =
    fullResult?.metadata?.classProbabilities ||
    fullResult?.classProbabilities;
  const classifierAi = classProbs
    ? Math.round(
        ((classProbs.ai ?? 0) +
          (classProbs['translated-ai'] ?? 0) +
          (classProbs['human-edited-ai'] ?? 0)) *
          100
      )
    : 0;

  const sentences = fullResult?.sentences || [];
  const aiSentenceCount = sentences.filter(
    (s) => s.verdict && (s.verdict.includes('ai') || s.verdict === 'mixed')
  ).length;
  const sentenceAiRatio =
    sentences.length > 0 ? (aiSentenceCount / sentences.length) * 100 : 0;

  const combinedAiSignal = baseAi + mixedSignal * 0.75;
  const hasVerifiableAiAssistance =
    verdict.includes('ai') ||
    verdict.includes('mixed') ||
    verdict === 'mostly-ai-human-edited' ||
    verdict === 'mostly-human-ai-assisted';

  const isHighConfidence =
    balanced?.confidenceLevel !== 'Low' && confidence >= 40;

  const isVerifiedHuman =
    balanced &&
    (verdict === 'likely-human' ||
      verdict === 'mostly-human' ||
      (balanced.human >= 75 && baseAi <= 15 && mixedSignal <= 15));

  const isStrictAi =
    !isVerifiedHuman &&
    (raw.aiScore >= 65 ||
      (isHighConfidence &&
        (classifierAi >= 35 ||
          baseAi >= 22 ||
          combinedAiSignal >= 24 ||
          (hasVerifiableAiAssistance &&
            (baseAi >= 16 || classifierAi >= 25 || mixedSignal >= 8)) ||
          sentenceAiRatio >= 20 ||
          verdict === 'likely-ai' ||
          verdict === 'mostly-ai-human-edited')));

  const isModerateAi =
    !isVerifiedHuman &&
    (raw.aiScore >= 38 ||
      (isHighConfidence &&
        (classifierAi >= 18 ||
          baseAi >= 15 ||
          combinedAiSignal >= 18 ||
          mixedSignal >= 12 ||
          sentenceAiRatio >= 10)));

  let strictAi;
  if (isStrictAi) {
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.15 + 10) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.5 + mixedSignal * 0.9 + 20)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(86, candidate));
  } else if (isModerateAi) {
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.1) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.25 + mixedSignal * 0.75 + 10)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(84, Math.max(52, candidate));
  } else {
    strictAi = Math.min(
      26,
      Math.max(raw.aiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 0.5 + mixedSignal * 0.3))
    );
  }

  if (balanced) {
    strictAi = Math.max(strictAi, baseAi);
  }
  strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(4, strictAi));
  const human = 100 - strictAi;
  const risk = strictAi >= 65 ? 'High' : strictAi >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';
  return { strictAi, human, risk };
}

console.log('User AI sample strict:', evalStrict('AI text', userTelemetryBalanced));
console.log('Human written sample strict:', evalStrict(humanSample, humanBalanced));

  const isHighConfidence =
    balanced?.confidenceLevel !== 'Low' && confidence >= 40;

  // High-Sensitivity Strict Mode:
  // Designed as an aggressive screen to decisively detect and score AI text high.
  // Flags clear AI, AI-assisted, and hybrid synthetic patterns.

    raw.aiScore >= 60 ||
    (isHighConfidence &&
      (classifierAi >= 35 ||
        baseAi >= 22 ||
        combinedAiSignal >= 24 ||
        (hasVerifiableAiAssistance &&
          (baseAi >= 16 || classifierAi >= 25 || mixedSignal >= 8)) ||
        sentenceAiRatio >= 20 ||
        verdict === 'likely-ai' ||
        verdict === 'mostly-ai-human-edited'));

  const isModerateAi =
    raw.aiScore >= 35 ||
    (isHighConfidence &&
      (classifierAi >= 18 ||
        baseAi >= 15 ||
        combinedAiSignal >= 18 ||
        mixedSignal >= 12 ||
        sentenceAiRatio >= 10));

  let strictAi: number;

  if (isStrictAi) {
    // Clear AI or assisted text is aggressively detected and scored high (86%–98% AI, High Risk)
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.15 + 10) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.5 + mixedSignal * 0.9 + 20)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(86, candidate));
  } else if (isModerateAi) {
    // Moderate/hybrid AI signals are strictly elevated to 52%–84%
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.1) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.25 + mixedSignal * 0.75 + 10)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(84, Math.max(52, candidate));
  } else {
    // Pure verified human writing with low balanced AI and human markers remains low
    strictAi = Math.min(
      26,
      Math.max(raw.aiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 0.5 + mixedSignal * 0.3))
    );
  }

  // Ensure invariant: strict AI can never be lower than verified balanced AI
  if (balancedResult) {
    strictAi = Math.max(strictAi, baseAi);
  }
  strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(4, strictAi));

  const isHighConfidence =
    balanced?.confidenceLevel !== 'Low' && confidence >= 40;

  // Check if balanced detector or linguistic engine verified natural human writing
  const isVerifiedHuman =
    (balanced &&
      (verdict === 'likely-human' ||
        verdict === 'mostly-human' ||
        (balanced.human >= 75 && baseAi <= 15 && mixedSignal <= 15))) ||
    (raw.aiScore < 30 &&
      raw.recommendations.some((r) =>
        r.includes('align with natural human writing')
      ) &&
      verdict !== 'likely-ai');

  // High-Sensitivity Strict Mode:
  // Designed as an aggressive screen to decisively detect and score AI text high.
  // Flags clear AI, AI-assisted, and hybrid synthetic patterns.
  // Preserves genuine human prose when verified natural human writing is detected.
  const isStrictAi =
    !isVerifiedHuman &&
    (raw.aiScore >= 65 ||
      (isHighConfidence &&
        (classifierAi >= 35 ||
          baseAi >= 22 ||
          combinedAiSignal >= 24 ||
          (hasVerifiableAiAssistance &&
            (baseAi >= 16 || classifierAi >= 25 || mixedSignal >= 8)) ||
          sentenceAiRatio >= 20 ||
          verdict === 'likely-ai' ||
          verdict === 'mostly-ai-human-edited')));

    !isVerifiedHuman &&

      (isHighConfidence &&
        (classifierAi >= 18 ||
          baseAi >= 15 ||
          combinedAiSignal >= 18 ||
          mixedSignal >= 12 ||
          sentenceAiRatio >= 10)));

  let strictAi: number;

  if (isStrictAi) {
    // Clear AI or assisted text is aggressively detected and scored high (86%–98% AI, High Risk)
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.15 + 10) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.5 + mixedSignal * 0.9 + 20)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(98, Math.max(86, candidate));
  } else if (isModerateAi) {
    // Moderate/hybrid AI signals are strictly elevated to 52%–84%
    const candidate = Math.max(
      raw.aiScore,
      classifierAi > 0 ? Math.round(classifierAi * 1.1) : 0,
      Math.round(baseAi * 1.25 + mixedSignal * 0.75 + 10)
    );
    strictAi = Math.min(84, Math.max(52, candidate));
  } else {
    // Verified or consistent human writing with low balanced AI remains low
    strictAi = Math.min(
      26,
      Math.max(raw.aiScore, Math.round(baseAi * 0.5 + mixedSignal * 0.3))
    );

  // Ensure invariant: strict AI can never be lower than verified balanced AI
  if (balancedResult) {
    strictAi = Math.max(strictAi, baseAi);
